Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the protocols object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 983. The moduli space has dimension at most 193 with 3 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_983(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 983. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^193(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a protocols algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 433. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 429/2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 433 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the protocols structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 429. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
